script

;
; Counters
;

	declare_persistent_counter first_time_setup
	declare_persistent_counter greek_wars_setup
	declare_persistent_counter punic_wars_setup
	declare_persistent_counter socii_wars_setup
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_rome_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_carthage_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_parthia_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_judea_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_greek_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_egypt_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_latin_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_desert_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_nuragic_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_slave_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter parthia_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter steppe_faction_04_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter steppe_faction_05_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter mercenary_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter arsaces_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter maccabee_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter caesar_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter slave_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rome_is_in_danger
	declare_persistent_counter rome_civil_war
	declare_persistent_counter carthage_civil_war
	declare_persistent_counter sos_julii_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_brutii_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_scipii_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_senate_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_parthia_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_caesar_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_slave_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_punic_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter sos_socii_spawned
	declare_persistent_counter rebel_purge
	declare_persistent_counter is_rome
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_muster_field_completed
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_militia_barracks_completed
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_city_barracks_completed
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_army_barracks_completed
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_royal_barracks_completed
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_greek_marker_added
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_roman_marker_added
	declare_persistent_counter re_barracks_italic_marker_added

;
; Setup
;

	if I_CompareCounter first_time_setup = 0
		set_counter first_time_setup 1

		; Set vassals

		console_command become_protector romans_senate latin_faction_02
		console_command become_protector romans_senate latin_faction_03
		console_command become_protector romans_senate latin_faction_04
		console_command become_protector caucasian_faction_01 hellenic_faction_10
		console_command become_protector egypt anatolian_faction_02
		console_command become_protector scythia steppe_faction_03

		; Set counters

		if I_LocalFaction romans_julii
		|| I_LocalFaction romans_brutii
		|| I_LocalFaction romans_scipii
			set_counter is_rome 1
		end_if

		for_each settlement in world
			if not SettlementBuildingExists = native
				console_command create_building local native
			end_if

			; Submod changes. First-time setup: assign an origin marker to each existing starting barracks.  Later
			; construction and upgrades are handled by SettlementBuildingFinished monitors below.
			if SettlementBuildingExists = muster_field
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = militia_barracks
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = city_barracks
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = army_barracks
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = royal_barracks
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_carthaginian
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_eastern
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_egyptian
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_greek
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_roman
				remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_italic

				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = tracian_illyrian
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = barbarian
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = carthaginian
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_carthaginian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = eastern
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_eastern
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = egyptian
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_egyptian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = greek
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_greek
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = roman
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_roman
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = italic
					add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_italic
				end_if
			end_if

			if SettlementBuildingExists = stables
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = cavalry_barracks
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = hippodrome
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = circus_maximus
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_barbarian
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_carthaginian
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_eastern
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_egyptian
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_greek
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_roman
				remove_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_italic

				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = tracian_illyrian
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = barbarian
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_barbarian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = carthaginian
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_carthaginian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = eastern
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_eastern
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = egyptian
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_egyptian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = greek
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_greek
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = roman
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_roman
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = italic
					add_hidden_resource local equestrian_built_by_italic
				end_if
			end_if

			if SettlementBuildingExists = practice_field
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = archery_range
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = catapult_range
			|| SettlementBuildingExists = siege_engineer
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_barbarian
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_carthaginian
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_eastern
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_egyptian
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_greek
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_roman
				remove_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_italic

				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = tracian_illyrian
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_tracian_illyrian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = barbarian
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_barbarian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = carthaginian
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_carthaginian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = eastern
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_eastern
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = egyptian
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_egyptian
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = greek
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_greek
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = roman
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_roman
				end_if
				if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = italic
					add_hidden_resource local missiles_built_by_italic
				end_if
			end_if

			if IsCapital
				add_hidden_resource local capital
			end_if

			add_hidden_resource local mercenary

			if SettlementName Sidon
			|| SettlementName Tyre
				add_hidden_resource local greece
			end_if
		end_for

		; Spawn non-family generals

		spawn_army
			faction macedon
			character Aristodemus of Megalopolis, named character, age 20, , x 137, y 49
			unit greek general's guard cavalry early, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
			unit greek levy pikemen, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
			unit greek levy pikemen, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
			unit greek archer, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
		end

			console_command give_trait "Timarchus of Miletus" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Onias of Edessa" AIBattleBonus 1
		end_if
		if not I_LocalFaction seleucid
			console_command give_trait "Patroclus of Macedonia" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Pleistarchus Antipatrid" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Demodamas of Apamea" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Heraclitus of Laodicea" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Nicanor of Seleuceia" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Philotas of Pella" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Molon of Arbela" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Andragoras of Hecatompylos" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Zeuxis of Macedonia" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Molon of Macedonia" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Nidintu-Bel of Babylonia" AIBattleBonus 1
		end_if
		if not I_LocalFaction hellenic_faction_08
			console_command give_trait "Theaetetus of Cyrene" AIBattleBonus 1
			console_command give_trait "Theodoros of Cyrene" AIBattleBonus 1
		end_if
		if not I_LocalFaction hellenic_faction_09
			console_command give_trait "Cleanthes of Assos" AIBattleBonus 1
		end_if
	end_if

;
; Check Rome SOS
; Handle Empty Garrisons
; Handle Senate
; Handle Adirim
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnEnd
		declare_counter still_alive

		if FactionIsLocal
			if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = AtWar
			and I_CompareCounter rome_is_in_danger = 0
				set_counter rome_is_in_danger 1
			end_if
		end_if

		if not FactionIsLocal
		and not FactionType slave

			; Calculate is_culture for empty cities

			declare_counter is_culture

			if FactionCultureType barbarian
				if FactionType rebel_slave
					set_counter is_culture 1

					if Treasury < -5000
					and LosingMoney
						console_command add_money local 10000
					end_if
				end_if

				; Britanic Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType britons
					|| FactionType britanic_faction_01
					|| FactionType britanic_faction_02
					|| FactionType britanic_faction_03
					|| FactionType britanic_faction_04
					|| FactionType britanic_faction_05
						set_counter is_culture 2
					end_if

					; Dacian Factions
					if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
						if FactionType dacia
						|| FactionType dacian_faction_01
						|| FactionType dacian_faction_02
						|| FactionType dacian_faction_03
							set_counter is_culture 3
						end_if

						; Germanic Factions
						if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
							if FactionType germans
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_01
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_02
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_03
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_04
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_05
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_06
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_07
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_08
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_09
							|| FactionType germanic_faction_10
								set_counter is_culture 5
							end_if

							; Iberian Factions
							if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
								if FactionType spain
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_01
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_02
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_03
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_04
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_05
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_06
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_07
								|| FactionType iberian_faction_08
								|| FactionType rebel_nuragic
									set_counter is_culture 6
								end_if

								; Scythian Factions
								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
									if FactionType scythia
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_01
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_02
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_03
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_04
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_05
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_06
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_07
									|| FactionType steppe_faction_08
										set_counter is_culture 7
									end_if

									; Gallic Factions
									if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
										set_counter is_culture 4
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if

			if not FactionCultureType barbarian
				if FactionCultureType greek
					set_counter is_culture 8
				end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionCultureType carthaginian

						; Desert Factions
						set_counter is_culture 9

						if FactionType carthage
						|| FactionType carthage_barcid
						|| FactionType carthage_hannonid
						|| FactionType carthage_magonid
						|| FactionType rebel_carthage
							set_counter is_culture 6
						end_if
					end_if

					if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
						if FactionCultureType eastern
							set_counter is_culture 10
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
							if FactionCultureType tracian_illyrian
								set_counter is_culture 8

								if FactionType thracian_faction_02
									set_counter is_culture 3
								end_if
							end_if

							if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0

								; Roman and Italic Factions
								set_counter is_culture 11

								if FactionCultureType egyptian
									set_counter is_culture 12
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if

			for_each settlement in faction
				inc_counter still_alive 1

				; Only handle bonus troops when not besieged

				if SettlementOrderLevel besieged = 0
					if HasResource infantry
					and IsSettlementGarrisoned
					and RandomPercent > 50
						declare_counter is_upgrade

						for_each army in settlement
							if ArmyIsGarrison
								;;count the number of units in garrisons
								for_each unit in army
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType greek hoplite militia
											set_counter is_upgrade 1
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType carthaginian city militia
											set_counter is_upgrade 2
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType greek levy pikemen
											set_counter is_upgrade 3
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType roman city militia
											set_counter is_upgrade 4
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType east infantry
											set_counter is_upgrade 5
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType italian spearmen
											set_counter is_upgrade 6
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType egyptian nile infantry
											set_counter is_upgrade 7
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType barb infantry gaul
											set_counter is_upgrade 8
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType warband spear german
											set_counter is_upgrade 9
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType barb infantry dacian
											set_counter is_upgrade 10
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType numidian levy
											set_counter is_upgrade 11
										end_if
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 0
										if UnitType barb infantry briton
											set_counter is_upgrade 12
										end_if
										if UnitType egyptian nubian spearmen
											set_counter is_upgrade 13
										end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
									end_if
								end_for
							end_if
						end_for

						if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade > 0
							if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade <= 7
								if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade <= 4
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 1
										if not FactionCultureType tracian_illyrian
											console_command swap_unit local_settlement "greek hoplite militia" "greek hoplite" 1
										end_if

										if FactionCultureType tracian_illyrian
											if HasResource owner_illyrian
												console_command swap_unit local_settlement "greek hoplite militia" "warband spear illyrian" 1
											end_if

											if HasResource owner_thracian
												console_command swap_unit local_settlement "greek hoplite militia" "warband falx thracian" 1
											end_if
										end_if
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 2
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "carthaginian city militia" "carthaginian infantry" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 3
										if FactionCultureType greek
											console_command swap_unit local_settlement "greek levy pikemen" "greek pikemen" 1
										end_if

										if not FactionCultureType greek
											console_command swap_unit local_settlement "greek levy pikemen" "greek hoplite" 1
										end_if
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 4
										if not MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command swap_unit local_settlement "roman city militia" "roman hastati" 1
										end_if

										if MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command swap_unit local_settlement "roman city militia" "roman infantry auxillia" 1
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade > 4
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 5
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "east infantry" "east hillmen" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 6
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "italian spearmen" "samnite" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 7
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "egyptian nile infantry" "egyptian infantry" 1
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if

							if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade > 7
								if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade <= 10
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 8
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "barb infantry gaul" "warband sword gaul" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 9
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "warband spear german" "warband axe german" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 10
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "barb infantry dacian" "warband falx dacian" 1
									end_if
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade > 10
									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 11
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "numidian levy" "berber axemen" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 12
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "barb infantry briton" "warband sword briton" 1
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_upgrade = 13
										console_command swap_unit local_settlement "egyptian nubian spearmen" "nile infantry" 1
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if
					end_if

					; When not garrisoned, spawn peasants

					if not IsSettlementGarrisoned
						if I_CompareCounter is_culture <= 6
							if I_CompareCounter is_culture <= 3
								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 1
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant slave" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 2
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant briton" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 3
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant dacian" 1
								end_if
							end_if

							if I_CompareCounter is_culture > 3
								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 4
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant gaul" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 5
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant german" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 6
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian peasant" 1
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_culture > 6
							if I_CompareCounter is_culture <= 9
								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 7
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb peasant scythian" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 8
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "greek peasant" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 9
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "african peasant" 1
								end_if
							end_if

							if I_CompareCounter is_culture > 9
								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 10
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "east peasant" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 11
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman peasant" 1
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 12
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "egyptian peasant" 1
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if
			end_for

			if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1

				; Handle Rome SOS

				if FactionType romans_senate
					if not I_LocalFaction romans_julii
					and IsAlly romans_julii
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii >= 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii >= 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii >= 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "romans_julii"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource italy
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local romans_senate
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if

					if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
					and not I_LocalFaction romans_brutii
					and IsAlly romans_brutii
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii >= 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii >= 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii >= 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii < 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "romans_brutii"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource italy
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local romans_senate
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if

					if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
					and not I_LocalFaction romans_scipii
					and IsAlly romans_scipii
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii >= 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii >= 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "romans_scipii"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource italy
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local romans_senate
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if

				if FactionType carthage
					if not I_LocalFaction carthage_barcid
					and IsAlly carthage_barcid
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid >= 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid >= 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid >= 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "carthage_barcid"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource punic
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local carthage
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if

					if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
					and not I_LocalFaction carthage_hannonid
					and IsAlly carthage_hannonid
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid >= 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid >= 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid >= 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid < 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "carthage_hannonid"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource punic
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local carthage
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if

					if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
					and not I_LocalFaction carthage_magonid
					and IsAlly carthage_magonid
						declare_counter is_vassal

						if I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 10
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid >= 10
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 5
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid >= 5
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_barcid < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_hannonid < 3
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal < 1
						and I_NumberOfSettlements carthage_magonid >= 3
							set_counter is_vassal 1
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_vassal = 1
							for_each settlement in faction "carthage_magonid"
								if I_CompareCounter still_alive < 1
								and HasResource punic
								and not IsCapital
									console_command capture_settlement local carthage
									console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian peasant" 1

									add_hidden_resource local capital
									add_hidden_resource local mercenary

									inc_counter still_alive 1
								end_if
							end_for
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

;
; Rebel Purge
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnEnd FactionType slave
		if I_CompareCounter rebel_purge < 10
			inc_counter rebel_purge 1
		end_if

		if FactionIsAlive rebel_slave
			if I_CompareCounter rebel_purge = 4
			|| I_CompareCounter rebel_purge = 8
				for_each character in faction
					if AgentType = merchant
					and RandomPercent > 50
						console_command kill_character local
					end_if

					if IsGeneral
					and not IsFactionLeader
					and not EndedInSettlement
					and not AtSea
						if AgentType = named character
							console_command give_trait local Immovable 2
							console_command change_character_faction local_character rebel_slave
						end_if

						if not AgentType = named character
							console_command kill_army local dont_show_death_anim
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter rebel_purge >= 10
				set_counter rebel_purge 1

				; Handle Defection

				for_each faction in world
					if not FactionType slave
					and not FactionType rebel_slave
						for_each character in faction
							if AgentType = named character
							and not IsFactionLeader
							and not EndedInSettlement
							and not AtSea
								declare_counter is_traitor

								if Trait Corrupt > 0
								and RandomPercent > 75
									inc_counter is_traitor 1
								end_if

								if Trait PoliticsSkill > 2
								and RandomPercent > 75
									inc_counter is_traitor 1
								end_if

								if Trait DeceiverVirtue > 0
								and RandomPercent > 75
									inc_counter is_traitor 1
								end_if

								if Trait VictorRomanVirtue > 1
								and RandomPercent > 75
									inc_counter is_traitor 1

									if Trait VictorRomanVirtue > 2
									and RandomPercent > 75
										inc_counter is_traitor 1
									end_if
								end_if

								if Trait VictorOthersVirtue > 1
								and RandomPercent > 75
									inc_counter is_traitor 1

									if Trait VictorOthersVirtue > 2
									and RandomPercent > 75
										inc_counter is_traitor 1
									end_if
								end_if

								if Trait Disloyal > 0
									inc_counter is_traitor 1

									if Trait Disloyal > 1
										inc_counter is_traitor 1

										if Trait Disloyal > 2
											inc_counter is_traitor 1
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if

								if Trait Upright > 0
								and RandomPercent > 50
									inc_counter is_traitor -1

									if Trait Upright > 1
									and RandomPercent > 50
										inc_counter is_traitor -1

										if Trait Upright > 2
										and RandomPercent > 50
											inc_counter is_traitor -1
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if

								if Trait PlainRomanVirtue > 1
								and RandomPercent > 50
									inc_counter is_traitor -1
								end_if

								if Trait Loyal > 0
									inc_counter is_traitor -1

									if Trait Loyal > 1
										inc_counter is_traitor -1

										if Trait Loyal > 2
											inc_counter is_traitor -1

											if Trait Loyal > 3
												inc_counter is_traitor -1
											end_if
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if

								if IsFactionHeir
									inc_counter is_traitor -2
								end_if

								if RandomPercent > 50
									if HasAncillary aged_retainer
									|| HasAncillary praetorian_guardsman
									|| HasAncillary problem_mother
										inc_counter is_traitor -1
									end_if
								end_if

								if RandomPercent > 50
									if HasOffice Quaestor
									|| HasOffice Aedile
									|| HasOffice Praetor
									|| HasOffice Consul
									|| HasOffice Censor
									|| HasOffice PontifexMaximus
										inc_counter is_traitor -1
									end_if
								end_if

								if I_CompareCounter is_traitor > 0
									if RandomPercent > 97
										set_counter is_traitor 0
										console_command change_character_faction local_character rebel_slave
									end_if

									if I_CompareCounter is_traitor > 3
										if RandomPercent > 91
											set_counter is_traitor 0
											console_command change_character_faction local_character rebel_slave
										end_if

										if I_CompareCounter is_traitor > 7
											if RandomPercent > 85
												set_counter is_traitor 0
												console_command change_character_faction local_character rebel_slave
											end_if
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if

							; Kill Bribed Diplomats

							if Trait Dying > 0
								console_command kill_character local
							end_if
						end_for
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if
		end_if

		if not FactionIsAlive rebel_slave
			if I_CompareCounter rebel_purge >= 10
				set_counter rebel_purge 1

				for_each character in faction
					if IsGeneral
					and not AgentType = named character
					and not IsFactionLeader
					and not EndedInSettlement
					and not AtSea
					and RandomPercent > 50
						console_command kill_army local dont_show_death_anim
					end_if

					if AgentType = merchant
					and RandomPercent > 50
						console_command kill_character local
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

	; Faction Death Script

	monitor_event HordeFormed
		if not FactionIsLocal
			; Check for living family members
			declare_counter num_family

			for_each character in faction
				if AgentType = named character
					inc_counter num_family 1

					console_command give_trait local Immovable 2
				end_if
			end_for

			; When no living family kill the faction
			if I_CompareCounter num_family = 0
				for_each character in faction
					console_command kill_character local
				end_for
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

;
; Submod changes. Carthaginian Barracks Conversion
;

	; Track the culture that last built or upgraded every normal barracks level.  A marker is stored
	; as a hidden resource on the settlement: barracks_built_by_<culture>.  Before assigning a new
	; marker, remove all eight culture markers so each settlement has at most one barracks origin.
	; The same operation is performed during first-time setup for starting barracks in a new campaign.
	;
	; export_descr_buildings.txt permits a Carthaginian conversion only when the settlement does not
	; have barracks_built_by_carthaginian.  Therefore a Carthaginian player can convert captured
	; foreign barracks, but cannot convert barracks last built or upgraded by a Carthaginian culture.
	; Conversion completion explicitly replaces the foreign marker with the Carthaginian one, so a
	; console-created replacement cannot leave the conversion available a second time.

	monitor_event BuildingCompleted SettlementBuildingFinished = muster_field
		inc_counter re_barracks_muster_field_completed 1
		console_command check_persistent_var re_barracks_muster_field_completed
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_tracian_illyrian
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_carthaginian
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_eastern
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_egyptian
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_greek
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_roman
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_italic

		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = tracian_illyrian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_tracian_illyrian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = barbarian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = carthaginian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_carthaginian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = eastern
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_eastern
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = egyptian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_egyptian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = greek
			inc_counter re_barracks_greek_marker_added 1
			console_command check_persistent_var re_barracks_greek_marker_added
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_greek
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = roman
			inc_counter re_barracks_roman_marker_added 1
			console_command check_persistent_var re_barracks_roman_marker_added
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_roman
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = italic
			inc_counter re_barracks_italic_marker_added 1
			console_command check_persistent_var re_barracks_italic_marker_added
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_italic
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event BuildingCompleted SettlementBuildingFinished = militia_barracks
		inc_counter re_barracks_militia_barracks_completed 1
		console_command check_persistent_var re_barracks_militia_barracks_completed
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_tracian_illyrian
		remove_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian

		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = barbarian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_barbarian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = carthaginian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_carthaginian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = eastern
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_eastern
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = egyptian
			add_hidden_resource local barracks_built_by_egyptian
		end_if
		if I_SettlementOwnerCulture local = greek

;
; Rebel Spawn
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType rebel_romans
		if I_CompareCounter rebel_rome_spawned = 0
			set_counter rebel_rome_spawned 1

			for_each character in faction
				if IsFactionLeader
					console_command give_trait local Fertile 3
					console_command give_trait local HaleAndHearty 3
					console_command give_trait local BattleScarred 2
					console_command give_trait local HighPersonalSecurity 3

					if FactionIsLocal
						console_command go_to_char local
					end_if
				end_if

				if AgentType = named character
					console_command give_trait local roman 1

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
					and RandomPercent > 80
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 3
					end_if

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
					and RandomPercent > 66
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 2
					end_if

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 1
					end_if
				end_if
			end_for

			console_command add_money rebel_romans, 10000
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType rebel_carthage
		if I_CompareCounter rebel_carthage_spawned = 0
			set_counter rebel_carthage_spawned 1

			declare_counter is_not_ready

			if I_TurnNumber < 100
				for_each faction in world
					if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
						if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 95, 51
							set_counter is_not_ready 1
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for

				if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
					spawn_army
						faction slave
						character Hamilcar Barca, named character, age 20, , x 95, y 51
						unit carthaginian general's cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc libyan infantry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc libyan infantry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc libyan infantry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc libyan infantry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc numidian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc numidian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc numidian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc balearic slingers, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit merc balearic slingers, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
					end

					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" punic 1
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" LoyaltyStarter 1
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" NaturalMilitarySkill 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" GoodCommander 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" GoodAttacker 1
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" GoodDefender 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" GoodAmbusher 1
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" HaleAndHearty 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" Brave 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" BattleScarred 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" Energetic 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" Intelligent 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" TacticalSkill 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" LogisticalSkill 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" HatesRoman 2
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" HighPersonalSecurity 3
					console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" NightBattleCapable 1
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" mercenary_captain
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" drillmaster
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" chirugeon
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" swordbearer
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" bodyguard
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" heroic_saviour
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" wrestler
					console_command give_ancillary "Hamilcar Barca" priest_of_Asklepios

					if not I_LocalFaction rebel_carthage
						console_command give_trait "Hamilcar Barca" AIBattleBonus 1
					end_if

					console_command change_character_faction "Hamilcar Barca" rebel_carthage

					console_command set_heir "Hamilcar Barca"
				end_if
			end_if

			for_each character in faction
				if IsFactionLeader
					console_command give_trait local Fertile 3
					console_command give_trait local HaleAndHearty 3
					console_command give_trait local BattleScarred 2
					console_command give_trait local HighPersonalSecurity 3
					
					if FactionIsLocal

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType rebel_slave
		if FactionIsLocal
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_CompareCounter slave_spawned = 0
			for_each settlement in faction
				console_command capture_settlement local slave
				console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb infantry slave" 2
			end_for

			if I_CompareCounter rebel_slave_spawned >= 5
				set_counter rebel_slave_spawned 0

				if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction slave = Allied
					if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction slave = AtWar
						console_command diplomatic_stance rebel_slave slave neutral
					end_if

					console_command diplomatic_stance rebel_slave slave allied
				end_if
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter rebel_slave_spawned > 0
				inc_counter rebel_slave_spawned 1
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter rebel_slave_spawned = 0
				declare_counter is_check
				declare_counter is_jubal
				declare_counter is_moses
				declare_counter is_nabonidus
				declare_counter is_skaris

				for_each character in faction
					if AgentType = named character
						if CharacterName Jubal of Cyrene
							inc_counter is_check 1
							set_counter is_jubal 1
						end_if

						if CharacterName Moses of Fars
							inc_counter is_check 1
							set_counter is_moses 1
						end_if

						if CharacterName Nabukudracara of Uruk
							inc_counter is_check 1
							set_counter is_nabonidus 1
						end_if

						if CharacterName Skaris of Scodra
							inc_counter is_check 1
							set_counter is_skaris 1
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for

				if I_CompareCounter is_check < 4
					declare_counter is_check2

					for_each faction in world
						if I_CompareCounter is_check > is_check2
							if I_CompareCounter is_jubal = 0
								if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 1, 1
									inc_counter is_check2 1
									set_counter is_jubal 1
								end_if
							end_if
							if I_CompareCounter is_moses = 0
								if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 253, 1
									inc_counter is_check2 1
									set_counter is_moses 1
								end_if
							end_if
							if I_CompareCounter is_nabonidus = 1
								if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 194, 150
									inc_counter is_check2 1
									set_counter is_nabonidus 1
								end_if
							end_if
							if I_CompareCounter is_skaris = 1
								if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 191, 151
									inc_counter is_check2 1
									set_counter is_skaris 1
								end_if
							end_if
						end_if
					end_for

					if I_CompareCounter is_check >= is_check2
						if I_CompareCounter is_jubal = 0
							spawn_army
								faction slave
								character Jubal of Cyrene, named character, age 16, , x 1, y 1
								unit tribal warlord, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc libyan infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc libyan infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc numidian cavalry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
							end

							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" berber 1
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" LoyaltyStarter 1
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" AIBattleBonus 1
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" Immovable 2
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" NaturalMilitarySkill 2
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" GoodCommander 1
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" Brave 1
							console_command give_trait "Jubal of Cyrene" Loyal 4

							console_command change_character_faction "Jubal of Cyrene" rebel_slave
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_moses = 0
							spawn_army
								faction slave
								character Moses of Fars, named character, age 16, , x 253, y 1
								unit numidian general's cavalry early, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc eastern infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc eastern infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc arab cavalry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
							end

							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" arab 1
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" LoyaltyStarter 1
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" AIBattleBonus 1
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" Immovable 2
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" NaturalMilitarySkill 2
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" GoodCommander 1
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" Brave 1
							console_command give_trait "Moses of Fars" Loyal 4

							console_command change_character_faction "Moses of Fars" rebel_slave
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_nabonidus = 0
							spawn_army
								faction slave
								character Nabukudracara of Uruk, named character, age 16, , x 253, y 5
								unit east generals cavalry early, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc eastern infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc eastern infantry, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc armoured syrian elephant, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
							end

							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" mesopotamian 1
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" LoyaltyStarter 1
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" AIBattleBonus 1
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" Immovable 2
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" NaturalMilitarySkill 2
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" GoodCommander 1
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" Brave 1
							console_command give_trait "Nabukudracara of Uruk" Loyal 4

							console_command change_character_faction "Nabukudracara of Uruk" rebel_slave
						end_if

						if I_CompareCounter is_skaris = 0
							spawn_army
								faction slave
								character Skaris of Scodra, named character, age 16, , x 191, y 151
								unit barb chieftain cavalry early scythian, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc barb archer, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc barb archer, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
								unit merc horse archers, exp 1 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
							end

							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" s_paganism 1
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" LoyaltyStarter 1
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" AIBattleBonus 1
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" Immovable 2
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" NaturalMilitarySkill 2
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" GoodCommander 1
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" Brave 1
							console_command give_trait "Skaris of Scodra" Loyal 4

							console_command change_character_faction "Skaris of Scodra" rebel_slave
						end_if

						set_counter rebel_slave_spawned 1

						console_command add_money rebel_slave, 25000
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType parthia
		if I_CompareCounter parthia_spawned = 0
			set_counter parthia_spawned 1

			declare_counter is_not_ready

			if I_TurnNumber < 60
				for_each faction in world
					if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
						if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 254, 78
							set_counter is_not_ready 1
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for

				if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
					spawn_army
						faction slave
						character Phriapites of Asaak, named character, age 20, , x 254, y 78
						unit east generals cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit persian swordsmen, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit persian swordsmen, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit persian swordsmen, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east persian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east persian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east persian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east persian cavalry, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east heavy cataphract, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
						unit east camel cataphract, exp 0 armour 0 weapon_lvl 0
					end

					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" zoroastrian 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" LoyaltyStarter 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" NaturalMilitarySkill 2
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" HaleAndHearty 3
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" Intelligent 2
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" Austere 2
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" Energetic 2
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" LogisticalSkill 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" GoodTaxman 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" GoodTrader 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" GoodBuilder 1
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" HighPersonalSecurity 2
					console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" NightBattleCapable 1
					console_command give_ancillary "Phriapites of Asaak" chirugeon

					if not I_LocalFaction parthia
						console_command give_trait "Phriapites of Asaak" AIBattleBonus 1
					end_if

					console_command change_character_faction "Phriapites of Asaak" parthia

					console_command set_heir "Phriapites of Asaak"
				end_if
			end_if

			for_each character in faction
				if IsFactionLeader
					console_command give_trait local Fertile 3
					console_command give_trait local HaleAndHearty 3
					console_command give_trait local BattleScarred 2
					console_command give_trait local HighPersonalSecurity 3
					
					if FactionIsLocal
						console_command go_to_char local
					end_if
				end_if

				if AgentType = named character
					console_command give_trait local zoroastrian 1

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
					and RandomPercent > 80
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 3
					end_if

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
					and RandomPercent > 66
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 2
					end_if

					if Trait NaturalMilitarySkill < 1
						console_command give_trait local NaturalMilitarySkill 1
					end_if
				end_if
			end_for

			console_command add_money parthia, 10000
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType steppe_faction_04
		if I_CompareCounter steppe_faction_04_spawned = 0

	monitor_event SettlementTurnEnd
		if not FactionType rebel_slave
		and not FactionType slave

			; Calculate is_culture to identify owner

			declare_counter is_culture

			if FactionCultureType barbarian

				; Britanic Factions
				if FactionType britons
				|| FactionType britanic_faction_01
				|| FactionType britanic_faction_02
				|| FactionType britanic_faction_03
				|| FactionType britanic_faction_04
				|| FactionType britanic_faction_05
					set_counter is_culture 1
				end_if

				; Dacian Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType dacia
					|| FactionType dacian_faction_01
					|| FactionType dacian_faction_02
					|| FactionType dacian_faction_03
						set_counter is_culture 2
					end_if

				; Germanic Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType germans
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_01
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_02
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_03
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_04
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_05
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_06
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_07
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_08
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_09
					|| FactionType germanic_faction_10
						set_counter is_culture 3
					end_if

				; Celtiberian Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType spain
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_01
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_02
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_07
						set_counter is_culture 4
					end_if

				; Iberian Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType iberian_faction_03
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_04
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_05
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_06
					|| FactionType iberian_faction_08
						set_counter is_culture 5
					end_if

				; Scythian Factions
				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0
					if FactionType scythia
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_01
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_02
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_03
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_04
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_05
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_06
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_07
					|| FactionType steppe_faction_08
						set_counter is_culture 6
					end_if

				; Gallic Factions

					set_counter is_culture 7

					if FactionType rebel_nuragic

				end_if

				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
			end_if

			if not FactionCultureType barbarian

					set_counter is_culture 9

					if FactionType hellenic_faction_10
						set_counter is_culture 10
					end_if
				end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_culture = 0

							end_if

									set_counter is_culture 1

				and not HasResource convert4
					set_counter is_check 1
					add_hidden_resource local convert1
					add_hidden_resource local cv20
				end_if

					if not HasResource cv1

							set_counter is_check 1
							remove_hidden_resource local cv20
							add_hidden_resource local cv19

; Handle Extermination

	monitor_event ExterminatePopulation
		if HasResource exterminated
			remove_hidden_resource local exterminated
			remove_hidden_resource local ex1
			remove_hidden_resource local ex2
			remove_hidden_resource local ex3
			remove_hidden_resource local ex4
			remove_hidden_resource local ex5
			remove_hidden_resource local ex6
			remove_hidden_resource local ex7
			remove_hidden_resource local ex8
			remove_hidden_resource local ex9
			remove_hidden_resource local ex10
			remove_hidden_resource local ex11
			remove_hidden_resource local ex12
			remove_hidden_resource local ex13
			remove_hidden_resource local ex14
			remove_hidden_resource local ex15
			remove_hidden_resource local ex16
			remove_hidden_resource local ex17
			remove_hidden_resource local ex18
			remove_hidden_resource local ex19
			remove_hidden_resource local ex20
		end_if

		add_hidden_resource local exterminated
		add_hidden_resource local ex20
	end_monitor

	monitor_event SettlementTurnEnd
		if HasResource exterminated
			declare_counter is_check

			if HasResource ex20
				set_counter is_check 1
				remove_hidden_resource local ex20
				add_hidden_resource local ex19
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex19
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex19
					add_hidden_resource local ex18
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex18
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex18
					add_hidden_resource local ex17
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex17
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex17
					add_hidden_resource local ex16
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex16
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex16
					add_hidden_resource local ex15
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex15
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex15
					add_hidden_resource local ex14
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex14
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex14
					add_hidden_resource local ex13
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex13
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex13
					add_hidden_resource local ex12
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex12
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex12
					add_hidden_resource local ex11
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex11
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex11
					add_hidden_resource local ex10
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex10
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex10
					add_hidden_resource local ex9
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex9
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex9
					add_hidden_resource local ex8
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex8
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex8
					add_hidden_resource local ex7
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex7
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex7
					add_hidden_resource local ex6
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex6
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex6
					add_hidden_resource local ex5
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex5
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex5
					add_hidden_resource local ex4
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex4
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex4
					add_hidden_resource local ex3
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex3
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex3
					add_hidden_resource local ex2
				end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex2
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex2
					add_hidden_resource local ex1
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_check = 0
				if HasResource ex1
					set_counter is_check 1
					remove_hidden_resource local ex1
					remove_hidden_resource local exterminated
				end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
			end_if
		end_if

		if HasResource capital
		and not IsCapital
			remove_hidden_resource local capital
		end_if

		if IsCapital
		and not HasResource capital
			add_hidden_resource local capital
		end_if

		if HasResource poverty
		and Treasury > 5000
			remove_hidden_resource local poverty
		end_if

		if Treasury < 1000
		and LosingMoney
		and not HasResource poverty
			add_hidden_resource local poverty
		end_if

		if not HasResource mercenary
			if FactionIsLocal
			and RandomPercent > 75
				add_hidden_resource local mercenary
			end_if

			if not FactionIsLocal
				declare_counter is_mercenary

				if FactionCultureType barbarian
					set_counter is_mercenary 1
					if RandomPercent > 90
						add_hidden_resource local mercenary
					end_if
				end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType greek
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 85
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType roman
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 95
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType egyptian
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 80
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType eastern
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 80
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType carthaginian
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 75
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType tracian_illyrian
						set_counter is_mercenary 1
						if RandomPercent > 85
							add_hidden_resource local mercenary
						end_if
					end_if

				if I_CompareCounter is_mercenary = 0
					if FactionCultureType italic
					and RandomPercent > 90
						add_hidden_resource local mercenary
					end_if
				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if

		if I_SettlementLevel local >= large_town
			if FactionIsLocal
				if HasResource cavalry
					if not SettlementBuildingExists = cavalry_barracks
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = hippodrome
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = circus_maximus
						remove_hidden_resource local cavalry
					end_if
				end_if

				if HasResource infantry
					if not SettlementBuildingExists = city_barracks
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = army_barracks
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = royal_barracks
						remove_hidden_resource local infantry
					end_if
				end_if

				if HasResource engine
					if not SettlementBuildingExists = catapult_range
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = siege_engineer
						remove_hidden_resource local engine
					end_if
				end_if

				if HasResource navy
					if not SettlementBuildingExists = port
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = shipwright
					and not SettlementBuildingExists = dockyard
						remove_hidden_resource local navy
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if

			if not HasResource cavalry
				if SettlementBuildingExists = cavalry_barracks
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = hippodrome
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = circus_maximus
					add_hidden_resource local cavalry
				end_if
			end_if

			if not HasResource infantry
				if SettlementBuildingExists = city_barracks
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = army_barracks
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = royal_barracks
					add_hidden_resource local infantry
				end_if
			end_if

			if not HasResource engine
				if SettlementBuildingExists = catapult_range
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = siege_engineer
					if not FactionCultureType italic
					and not HasResource owner_gallic
					and not HasResource owner_german
					and not HasResource owner_briton
						add_hidden_resource local engine
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if

			if not HasResource navy
				if SettlementBuildingExists = port
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = shipwright
				|| SettlementBuildingExists = dockyard
					add_hidden_resource local navy
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

;
; AI Units
;

	monitor_event UnitTrained

		; Only handle bonus troops when not besieged

		if SettlementOrderLevel besieged = 0

			; Ship
			if not TrainedUnitCategory ship

				if UnitType merc barbarian infantry
				|| UnitType merc greek hoplites
				|| UnitType merc eastern infantry
				|| UnitType merc spanish infantry
				|| UnitType merc thracian
				|| UnitType merc samnite
				|| UnitType merc warband falx
				|| UnitType merc warband sword
				|| UnitType merc warband spear
				|| UnitType merc peltast
				|| UnitType merc libyan infantry
				|| UnitType merc barbarian cavalry
				|| UnitType merc arab cavalry
				|| UnitType merc numidian cavalry
				|| UnitType merc horse archers
				|| UnitType merc east horse archer
				|| UnitType merc bastarnae
				|| UnitType merc greek pikemen
				|| UnitType merc egyptian infantry
				|| UnitType merc barb naked fanatics
				|| UnitType merc tribal slingers
				|| UnitType merc barb archer
				|| UnitType merc east archer
				|| UnitType merc illyrian
				|| UnitType merc balearic slingers
				|| UnitType merc cretan archers
				|| UnitType merc rhodian slingers
				|| UnitType merc syrian archer
				|| UnitType merc bedouin archers
				|| UnitType merc bedouin
				|| UnitType merc elephants
				|| UnitType merc onagers
				|| UnitType merc sarmatian cavalry
				|| UnitType merc thracian militia cavalry
				|| UnitType merc median cavalry
				|| UnitType merc carthaginian medium cavalry
				|| UnitType merc italian cavalry
				|| UnitType merc cilician pirate
				|| UnitType merc guerilla fighters
				|| UnitType merc barb scythian hoplite
				|| UnitType merc east heavy infantry
				|| UnitType merc wolf warrior
				|| UnitType merc sarissa cavalry
				|| UnitType merc armoured syrian elephant
				|| UnitType merc thessalian cavalry
					remove_hidden_resource local mercenary
				end_if

				if not FactionIsLocal
				and not FactionType slave
				and not HasResource poverty

					; Chance for bonus troops

					if Treasury > 1000
					and not LosingMoney

						declare_counter is_garrison

						for_each army in settlement
							if ArmyIsGarrison
								for_each unit in army
									inc_counter is_garrison 1
								end_for
							end_if
						end_for

						if I_CompareCounter is_garrison <= 16

							; Mercenary
							declare_counter merc_assist

							if GovernorInResidence
							|| IsCapital
								if RandomPercent > 60
								|| SettlementBuildingExists = waystation

									; Infantry troops

									if HasResource infantry
										if FactionCultureType barbarian
											if HasResource owner_gallic
												inc_counter merc_assist 1
												console_command add_money local -400
												console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb warguard gaul" 1
											end_if

											if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
												if HasResource owner_german
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -400
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "warband dhaxe german" 1
												end_if

											if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
												if HasResource owner_dacian
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -400

													if not FactionType dacian_faction_01
														console_command create_unit local_settlement "barb warguard dacian" 1
													end_if

													if FactionType dacian_faction_01
														console_command create_unit local_settlement "greek bastarnae" 1
													end_if
												end_if

											if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
												if HasResource owner_iberian
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -400
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "spanish scutarii" 1
												end_if

											if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
												if HasResource owner_briton

												if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -300
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc eastern infantry" 1
												end_if
											end_if

										if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
											if HasResource berber
											|| HasResource punic
												inc_counter merc_assist 1
												console_command add_money local -300
												console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc libyan infantry" 1
											end_if

										if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
											if HasResource arab
												if RandomPercent > 50
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -300
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc egyptian infantry" 1
												end_if

												if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -300
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc eastern infantry" 1
												end_if
											end_if

										if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
											if HasResource egyptian
												inc_counter merc_assist 1
												console_command add_money local -300
												console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc egyptian infantry" 1
											end_if

										if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
											if HasResource s_paganism
												inc_counter merc_assist 1
												console_command add_money local -300
												console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc barb archer" 1
											end_if

										if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
											if HasResource judaism
												if RandomPercent > 50
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -300
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc peltast" 1
												end_if

												if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
													inc_counter merc_assist 1
													console_command add_money local -300
													console_command create_unit local_settlement "merc eastern infantry" 1
												end_if
											end_if
										end_if
										end_if
										end_if
										end_if
										end_if
										end_if
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if

							; Handicap

							if I_CompareCounter merc_assist = 0
								if HasResource infantry
								and RandomPercent > 60
									if HasResource owner_roman
										inc_counter merc_assist 1
										console_command add_money local -400

										if not MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman princeps" 1
										end_if

										if MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary cohort i" 1
										end_if
									end_if

									if HasResource owner_carthage
										inc_counter merc_assist 1
										console_command add_money local -400

										if not MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian medium infantry" 1
										end_if

										if MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
											console_command create_unit local_settlement "carthaginian heavy infantry" 1
										end_if
									end_if
								end_if
							end_if

							; Remove mercenary resource when mercenaries were hired

							if I_CompareCounter merc_assist > 0
							and HasResource mercenary
								remove_hidden_resource local mercenary
							end_if
						end_if
					end_if
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType egypt
		if FactionIsLocal
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction seleucid = AtWar

					console_command diplomatic_stance egypt seleucid neutral
				end_if

				console_command diplomatic_stance egypt seleucid war
			end_if
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType macedon
		if I_CompareCounter greek_wars_setup = 2
		and RandomPercent > 50
			set_counter greek_wars_setup 3
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_CompareCounter greek_wars_setup = 1
		and RandomPercent > 50
			if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction hellenic_faction_01 = AtWar
			|| DiplomaticStanceFromFaction hellenic_faction_09 = AtWar
				set_counter greek_wars_setup 2

				if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction egypt = AtWar
					if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction egypt = Allied
						console_command diplomatic_stance egypt macedon neutral
					end_if

					console_command diplomatic_stance egypt macedon war
				end_if

				if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction hellenic_faction_01 = AtWar

					end_if

				end_if

	end_monitor

					|| I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 101, 65

	; Carthage has started the 2nd punic War - Only start when Rome has taken land on Sicily
	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType carthage
		if I_CompareCounter punic_wars_setup = 5
		and I_TurnNumber > 140
		and not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction rebel_carthage = AtWar
		and RandomPercent > 50
			set_counter punic_wars_setup 6

			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		; Warn player of the 2nd Punic Wars

		if I_CompareCounter punic_wars_setup = 1
		and RandomPercent > 50
			declare_counter is_event

			if I_TurnNumber > 70
			and DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = AtWar
			and I_SettlementOwnerCulture Panormus = roman
			and I_SettlementOwnerCulture Akragas = roman
			and not I_SettlementOwner Panormus = rebel_latin
			and not I_SettlementOwner Akragas = rebel_latin
				set_counter is_event 1
			end_if

			if I_TurnNumber > 100
				set_counter is_event 1
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter is_event = 1
				declare_counter is_not_ready

				for_each faction in world
					if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
						if I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 100, 69
						|| I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 115, 64
						|| I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 116, 62
						|| I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 105, 67
							set_counter is_not_ready 1
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for

				if I_CompareCounter is_not_ready < 1
				and FactionIsAlive rebel_carthage
				and not I_LocalFaction rebel_carthage
					set_counter punic_wars_setup 2
				end_if
			end_if
		end_if

					|| I_FactionNearTile local_faction 0 98, 47

		if I_CompareCounter punic_wars_setup > 0
			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction seleucid = AtWar

				end_if
