;
; Rome Civil War
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_julii
		if not I_LocalFaction romans_julii
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_julii >= 30
		and DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = Allied
			set_counter rome_civil_war 1

			override_superfaction_popularity romans_julii true
			set_faction_people_standing romans_julii 100

			console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_senate neutral

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = AtWar
				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_senate war
			end_if

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_brutii = AtWar
				if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_brutii = Allied
					console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_brutii neutral
				end_if

				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_brutii war
			end_if

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_scipii = AtWar
				if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_scipii = Allied
					console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_scipii neutral
				end_if

				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_julii romans_scipii war
			end_if
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_brutii
		if not I_LocalFaction romans_brutii
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_brutii >= 30
		and DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = Allied
			set_counter rome_civil_war 1

			override_superfaction_popularity romans_brutii true
			set_faction_people_standing romans_brutii 100

			console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_senate neutral

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_senate = AtWar
				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_senate war
			end_if

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_julii = AtWar
				if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_julii = Allied
					console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_julii neutral
				end_if

				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_julii war
			end_if

			if not DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_scipii = AtWar
				if DiplomaticStanceFromFaction romans_scipii = Allied
					console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_scipii neutral
				end_if

				console_command diplomatic_stance romans_brutii romans_scipii war
			end_if
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_scipii
		if not I_LocalFaction romans_scipii
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_NumberOfSettlements romans_scipii >= 30

;
; Rome SOS when Rome is not Human
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_senate
		if I_CompareCounter rome_is_in_danger = 1
			terminate_monitor
		end_if

		if I_FactionBesieged romans_senate
			set_counter rome_is_in_danger 1
			terminate_monitor
		end_if
	end_monitor

;
; Spawn reinforcements during SOS
;

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_julii
		if I_CompareCounter rome_is_in_danger > 0
			if FactionIsLocal
			|| I_CompareCounter sos_julii_spawned >= 20
				terminate_monitor
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter sos_julii_spawned < 10
			and not MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
				for_each settlement in faction

					declare_counter is_garrison

					for_each army in settlement
						if ArmyIsGarrison
							for_each unit in army
								inc_counter is_garrison 1
							end_for
						end_if
					end_for

					if I_CompareCounter is_garrison <= 14
						if HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 75
							inc_counter sos_julii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman princeps" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman triarii" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman velite" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman hastati" 3
							console_command add_money romans_julii, 10000
						end_if

						if not HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 95
							inc_counter sos_julii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman princeps" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman triarii" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman velite" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman hastati" 3
							console_command add_money romans_julii, 10000
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if

			if MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
			and I_CompareCounter sos_julii_spawned < 20
				for_each settlement in faction

					declare_counter is_garrison

					for_each army in settlement
						if ArmyIsGarrison
							for_each unit in army
								inc_counter is_garrison 1
							end_for
						end_if
					end_for

					if I_CompareCounter is_garrison <= 14
						if HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 75
							inc_counter sos_julii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary cohort i" 3
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary first cohort i" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman light infantry auxillia" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman cavalry auxillia" 1
							console_command add_money romans_julii, 10000
						end_if

						if not HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 95
							inc_counter sos_julii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary cohort i" 3
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary first cohort i" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman light infantry auxillia" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman cavalry auxillia" 1
							console_command add_money romans_julii, 10000
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor

	monitor_event FactionTurnStart FactionType romans_brutii
		if I_CompareCounter rome_is_in_danger > 0
			if FactionIsLocal
			|| I_CompareCounter sos_brutii_spawned >= 20
				terminate_monitor
			end_if

			if I_CompareCounter sos_brutii_spawned < 10
			and not MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
				for_each settlement in faction

					declare_counter is_garrison

					for_each army in settlement
						if ArmyIsGarrison
							for_each unit in army
								inc_counter is_garrison 1
							end_for
						end_if
					end_for

					if I_CompareCounter is_garrison <= 14
						if HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 75
							inc_counter sos_brutii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman princeps" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman triarii" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman velite" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman hastati" 3
							console_command add_money romans_brutii, 10000
						end_if

						if not HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 95
							inc_counter sos_brutii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman princeps" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman triarii" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman velite" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman hastati" 3
							console_command add_money romans_brutii, 10000
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if

			if MajorEventActive "marian_reforms"
			and I_CompareCounter sos_brutii_spawned < 20
				for_each settlement in faction

					declare_counter is_garrison

					for_each army in settlement
						if ArmyIsGarrison
							for_each unit in army
								inc_counter is_garrison 1
							end_for
						end_if
					end_for

					if I_CompareCounter is_garrison <= 14
						if HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 75
							inc_counter sos_brutii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary cohort i" 3
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary first cohort i" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman light infantry auxillia" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman cavalry auxillia" 1
							console_command add_money romans_brutii, 10000
						end_if

						if not HasResource italy
						and RandomPercent > 95
							inc_counter sos_brutii_spawned 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary cohort i" 3
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman legionary first cohort i" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman light infantry auxillia" 1
							console_command create_unit local_settlement "roman cavalry auxillia" 1
							console_command add_money romans_brutii, 10000
						end_if
					end_if
				end_for
			end_if
		end_if
	end_monitor
